### 0.9 ⛔⛔ New from **C2W5** — the read-protocol wave (filed 2026-08-01 by `doc-curator-c2w5-fold`; **this is their permanent home**)

> ⛔⛔ **THE MANDATORY RE-LABELLING COMES FIRST BECAUSE IT GOVERNS EVERYTHING ELSE IN THIS SECTION.** Charter **§A20.2** (Head + Advisor, 2026-08-01, **binding on registries, the ledger, the primers, this matrix and every future draft**): **any artifact saying *"the compositional family is refuted"* must instead say *"the `P`-particle occupancy read protocol is refuted at `P = 4`."*** ⛔ **And the physics is NOT adjudicated — the organizer swap was never reached.** ⚠ **Sweep status: one live site remains, in TRACKED CODE** (`chlu/experiments/exp_null_arms.py` line 13's docstring) — flagged to the Hub, not curator-editable.

**The tier-ii guards — the wave's own measurements.**

- ⛔⛔ **"THE COMPOSITIONAL FAMILY IS REFUTED" IN ANY FORM.** ⭐ **The family is SOLVABLE from the very inputs every arm saw**: a combinatorial matched filter over 35 960 set codes recovers `A(x)` exactly on **0.2719 ± 0.0126** of unseen queries as launched, and **1.0000 ± 0.0000** from the noiseless code. **What failed is the read**: the `P = 4` launch occupies **2.202 ± 0.02** of the 4 required distinct wells, reaches `≥ F` on **5.0 %** of queries, and has **exact-set occupancy 0.0000 / 2 560**. (**N214/N215**; §A20.2.)
- ⛔⛔ **THE 0.272 CEILING QUOTED AS AN ARM'S BAR, OR AS A LIVE LAUNDER BAR.** ⭐ **It is always labelled a *reference ceiling from an out-of-class decoder*** — what the launch information supports. **The arm bar is chance + 0.05 = 0.05039, at reader class ≤ `N_a·m = 256`.** ⚠ **And for the read-fix iteration, the Advisor's amendment A1 binds: the launch-only launder is RECOMPUTED LIVE on the task's own learned launches (store deleted, same reader class, same k); 0.272 is a reference line only, never the live bar.** (§A20.2/§A20.3(c).)
- ⛔⛔ **"NO MATCHED-CAPACITY ORGANIZER CLEARS" WITHOUT THE 0.272 CEILING IN THE SAME SENTENCE** (Hub ruling, standing). The two together are a design brief; the first alone is an epitaph. (**N215**.)
- ⛔⛔ **"PHYSICS LOSES" / any tier-ii null from the cat test.** ⭐ **The ONLY quotable form is *"physics, untuned and collapsed, does not clear its own K5."*** The arm read **0.0008 ± 0.0008** vs chance **0.0004** and **so did every control** (K5 margin **0.0000 on all 4 readers**); it ran **COLLAPSED** (`S_eff` 34.1/51.2/36.6 vs band [8,16], 3/3) and **UNTUNED** (registered grid NOT-RUN). ⛔ **A PRE-CONDITION kill, never a tier-ii null.** (**N212**.)
- ⛔ **"K3 ✅ K4 ✅" AS EVIDENCE THE FAMILY IS SOUND** → both pass **VACUOUSLY** (upper-bound controls that pass when the launder scores low; every number in the cell is ≈ 0). **The informative rows are K1 and K5.** (**N212**.)
- ⛔ **OCCUPANCY / CO-ACTIVATION PRECISION SCORED AGAINST `F/N_a` (= 0.125).** ⭐ **The admissible baseline is the BLANK STORE / raw launch geometry** — measured **launch 0.406 → settled 0.297**, i.e. ⛔ **the settle DESTROYS 27 % of the set information the launch already carries**, and lowers distinct-well occupancy **2.20 → 1.70**. # ⭐⭐ THE C3 DIVISION — CAMPAIGN 3 ACCOUNTING (opened at **v2.17**, 2026-08-13, by `doc-curator-c2w11-claims-matrix-fold` Part B; authority = **`.claude/advisor-head-c3-charter.md` → C3 ADDENDUM 1 §1 / §2 / §3**, Head-ratified 2026-08-13)

> ⛔⛔ **THE ACCOUNTING RULE, AND IT IS WHY THIS IS A DIVISION AND NOT ANOTHER `§0.x`.** Everything **above** this line is **CAMPAIGN 2** accounting — the `§0.1 … §0.14` never-quote series and rows `CM-1 … CM-41` — and ⛔ **not one character of it is edited, re-scored or re-versioned by this pass.** Everything **below** is **CAMPAIGN 3** accounting: sections numbered **`§C3.x`**, rows numbered **`CM-C3-n`**. ⛔ **C3 numbering NEVER continues the C2 series** (there is no "CM-42"), so a C2 row and a C3 row can never be conflated by a reader or by a grep. ⚠ **Inheritance is OFF in both directions: a C3 row never inherits a C2 scope qualifier, and a C2 row never inherits a C3 venue guardrail.**

> ⛔ **The score sentence carries across the campaign boundary unchanged: external benchmarks won on their own headline metric = ZERO.** ⛔ **C3W1 trained no ladder arm; nothing in this division is a performance result about the CLU.**

### §C3.1 ⛔⛔ New from **C3W1** — the venue wave's never-quotes (filed 2026-08-13 by `doc-curator-c2w11-claims-matrix-fold` Part B; **this is their permanent home**; authority = **C3 Add.1 §1/§2/§3**; sources `.claude/outputs/c3-benchmark-scout.md`, `.claude/outputs/c3-trackb-tripwire.md`, `.claude/outputs/c3-csf3-harness.md` and the `[C3W1-REVIEW]` / `[C3W1-RULINGS]` entries of `.claude/c3-handover.md`)

> ⛔⛔ **WHY THIS SECTION EXISTS, in the Head's own ruling (2026-08-13):** *"Guardrail (a) is a CLAIMS obligation, not a code detail. It lives in `claims_matrix.md` as a never-quote row — folded by the extended A8 spoke — with the loader assertion as belt-and-braces, never as the only enforcement."* ⛔ **Until this section landed the matrix was UNSAFE FOR ANY PAPER-WRITER** (C3 Add.1 §3): the whole C3 venue set was ruled and banked and **this file knew none of it.**
> ⛔ **Scope statements that govern every clause below:** **Track A = enwik8 primary · WT-103 secondary · PG-19 an INTERNAL retention instrument, ⛔ never an external comparison** · **Track B primary = CAMELS-US** · ⛔ **N-CMAPSS DS02 = an APPLICATION venue (company-research relevance, e.g. HEPA), NEVER a primary-claim venue** · ⛔ **no C3 performance number exists yet — no ladder arm has trained, and the interim `MATCHED_STATE_BYTE_BUDGET` binds nothing** · ⛔ **the toy compositional substrate stays DEMOTED into C3 (Add.16 §A44.1) — smoke/regression instrument only, never a claim venue.**
>
> **⛔ THE NEVER-QUOTES (C3 Add.1 folded entire).**
> - ⛔⛔ **(A) any N-CMAPSS number quoted WITHOUT the matched-bytes exemplar reference row `7.988` RMSE beside it, AND without the FNN-tie fact** (the tie is with **`FNN 7.89`** — `FNN` data-driven `[w, x_s]`, **7.89 ± 0.12**; ratio **1.0124**, i.e. **+1.24 %** — it trips the 2 % rule against that one reference). ⭐ **Disclosure is the defense: the reference row is PRINTED in every N-CMAPSS table, and the emitter is structurally incapable of omitting it.** ✅ **Admissible claim form: CAPABILITY / APPLICATION ONLY.** ⛔ ***"Beats SOTA on RUL"* is NEVER-QUOTE in any wording** (`c3-trackb-tripwire` §§49–50/§§313–371; C3 Add.1 §1).
> - ⛔ **(B) any implication that N-CMAPSS is where a primary claim is made.** Guardrail **(b)** fixes what the venue IS for: **the persistent-store-across-units question (C2W10's re-homed VALUE question — a fleet is its natural home) · the anytime curve · interpretable-store probes.** ⛔ **Nothing else is what this venue is for** (C3 Add.1 §1).
> - ⛔⛔ **(C) any CAFE-derived N-CMAPSS or C-MAPSS comparison, in EITHER direction — the embargo is ABSOLUTE.** Original NASA files only; **HEPA comparisons are INTERNAL-ONLY unless re-derived on corrected labels.** ⚠ **Cross-reference the banked CAFE label-bug finding: ⛔ no CAFE C-MAPSS number is externally comparable** (C3 Add.1 §1 guardrail (c)).
> - ⛔ **(D) the shipped SAC-SMA output quoted against the LSTM** — it is **CALIBRATION-PERIOD** (0.708 against Kratzert's split-sample **0.603**), not out-of-sample (`c3-trackb-tripwire` via `[C3W1-REVIEW]`).
> - ⛔ **(E) any CAMELS number computed on `maurer` forcing** — the shipped product is **measured DEFECTIVE** (`tmax == tmin` on **> 99 %** of days, **20/20** basins; 3 malformed headers) ⇒ ✅ **`daymet` forcing ONLY**, with a `tmax > tmin` stage-time assertion so a future product swap cannot reintroduce it.
> - ⛔ **(F) the CAMELS reference `S` quoted as the mean `0.72`** — **the currency is the MEDIAN `0.758`** (it is `dies_if`'s currency and the *highest* number in Kratzert Table 3). Licence **`cc-by-4.0`** (Zenodo **15529996**).
> - ⛔⛔ **(G) any state-byte figure quoted without BOTH its convention AND its dtype.** ✅ **The convention is TOTAL state bytes, AS-DEPLOYED dtype** — ⛔ **no dtype normalization; the fp32 store pays its real cost**; **`dtype_bytes` is DECLARED per row, never inferred.**
> - ⛔⛔ **(H) the scout's single-store `1,966,080 B` row quoted as *"the CLU's state"*** — it is a **single-store** row; pairing it against **layer-summed** rival totals (and mixing **float32** ours with **bf16** theirs) is the counting error that produced the interim ≈2 MB figure. ⚠ **The ceiling digit binds at the rival-ladder PREREG, not before, and that prereg comes to the Advisor BEFORE any ladder arm trains.**
> - ⛔⛔ **(I) dyn-eval's published `0.94` bpc placed beside a `40 M` number** — it is at **`277 M`** parameters (WT-103's 16.4 ppl likewise at ~257 M). ⛔ **A table doing so is a CATEGORY ERROR.** ⛔ **Dyn-eval is an ARM WE RUN, not a citation.**
> - ⛔ **(J) run-3 results quoted as budget-compliant ladder rows.** ✅ **Run 3 is EXEMPT by PRE-REGISTRATION, ⛔ not by re-ruling the budget:** a **`preregistered_continuation`** exemption, **fingerprint-verified bit-identical to run 2 except `erosion_partition`**, **printed in the ledger** (it annotates, never suppresses).
> - ⛔ **(K) PG-19 quoted as an external comparison** — it is an **INTERNAL retention instrument** and nothing else.
>
> ✅ **THE ONE POSITIVE THIS SECTION MUST ALSO CARRY, because it is criterion 4's FIRST measured survivor in six attempts:** CAMELS-US **`E = 0.2559`** at matched bytes vs **`S = 0.758`**, **`margin_rel = −0.6625`** against a **`−0.02`** threshold, **no fire against any of 14 references**, **no fire at 536× the byte budget**; mechanism **AS PRE-REGISTERED** — the target depends on **unobserved accumulated storage**. ⛔ **It is a VENUE-ADMISSIBILITY result, never a CLU result: no CLU arm ran.**
> ⚠ **BELT-AND-BRACES, STATED HERE BECAUSE IT IS THE POINT OF THE SECTION:** the loader-side / emitter-side assertions that enforce (A), (C) and (E) in code are **belt-and-braces, ⛔ NEVER the only enforcement.** A claims obligation that lives only in a loader dies the first time a number is copied into prose.
>

## 1. Canonical constants (cite identically everywhere; source = F5 note)
| object | canonical form |
|---|---|
| latch displacement | q∞ = q0 + εp0/(Mγ), infinite half-life (flat direction, γ>0) |
| Noether charge decay | exactly (1−γ)ⁿ |
| retention law | n₁/₂ ∝ μ⁻² (GMOR in spectral mass), valid εμ ≲ γ/2 |
| floor | 2ln2/(−ln(1−γ)), mass-independent, past crossover |
| crossover / EP | h* ≈ γ/2; exceptional point, √(h−h*) frequency onset |
| retention minimum | γ* ≈ 2εμ (critical damping) |
| volume | det J = (1−γ)^d; position-gated: det J = (1−γφ(q′))^d |
| mass nomenclature | **inertial M** vs **spectral μ** — never bare "mass" (Def-1/Def-2) |
| **D1 lr-partition (CORRECTED v2.0)** | **`η_m/(η_m + K·η_k)`** — **K-dependent**, where K = the number of items sharing the fast lever. K=2 → **1/21**, matched to **13 digits** (`clu-memory-architecture` §2 Prop 6, Toy E). ⛔ **The old two-lever form `1/11` is WRONG for any multi-item setting** — a shared fast lever receives a K-fold richer gradient, so **D1 scale-collapse worsens linearly in the number of items served**. Anyone reusing `anti-collapse-characterization` §5.1 must use the K-form. *(The registered `1/11` was a pre-registration that failed — P6b — and the corrected law is exact and re-derivable in two lines.)* |
| **per-address mass (Prop 6)** | With **K items carrying per-item masses** on one landscape, only the *overall scale* remains gauge; **all ratios are wake-visible and exactly learnable** (verified `\|d − d*\| = 2.2e-14`). ⇒ **masses must be PER-ADDRESS codebook entries, not a global model parameter.** ⚠ ~~As shipped, `m` is a global `log_mass` shared by every rollout — it is not an address component at all.~~ **UPDATED v2.1 (w20):** the prerequisite is shipped — per-launch **`mass_override`** exists (`mass-visible-objective`; default `None` = bit-identical); the full per-address codebook remains a change-list. ⚠ **Scope (w21, Prop F1 / N76): per-address masses are a SELECTION mechanism (which shell/timescale reads) — they store nothing.** |
| **designed capacity law (NEW v2.1, w20)** | **`K_max = 4·2^d`** exactly over d=2–8 (`log₂K_max = d+2`, **R²=1.0000**; 1024 items at d=8; blank control passing every cell; d=12/16 censored at the compute cap and excluded from fits). ⛔ **The packing bound `(1+2R/w)^d` is FALSIFIED as a law** (measured β=0.96/1.28/1.65 vs β=d; overestimates 12.7×→1.7e6×) — the canonical form is the repair: **`K_max = (2R/Δ_req)^{d_eff}`** with **`Δ_req ≈ 3.1·max(w, σ_query)`** (d-independent resolution floor) and **`d_eff ≈ 0.72–0.83·d`** (predicts the measured base 2.964 to ~10%). ⚠ DESIGNED landscape only; the d=2 *prefactor* is precision-sensitive (f32→16, f64→8), the exponent robust. **Every quotation carries the bits-per-parameter denominator (N77: ≈1.1–1.6 vs the transformer's 2)** — the exponential is in address dimension, not parameters (`address-space-dimension-scaling`; N70) |
| **write-operator interference law (NEW v2.1, w21)** | Interference at a stored item is governed by the **C3 ratio `‖∇δV(q*)‖ / λ_min(Hess V(q*))`, evaluated at the RELAXED FIXED POINT** (not the read's launch point) — measured across 6 function classes and 4.6 decades, meas/pred ∈ [0.73,1.63] for 5/6 (support radius falsified, Spearman 0.143). Two safety routes: **local/masked write** (70× at identical class/params; drift `exp(−d²/2s²)`; advantage **amplifies with d: 197×→599×→6332×** at d=2/3/4) or **stiff wells** (13–29×); **anchored write (codebook rehearsal) = 103×, the only arm that both preserves AND stores.** ⛔ An admission gate on a learned landscape can only REFUSE (never quote "~5 orders" for a gate; N74) — on a **designed** store it is decisive (retention 1.000 vs 0.16) at a quoted capacity price (6.0±0.9/16 admitted ≈ packing 6.1) (`potential-function-class` §3–4, `sequential-write-interference` §0–1, `designed-mechanism-learned-content` recon 3) |
| **fiber composition + ceiling (NEW v2.1, w21 — Prop F1)** | Every read depends on `(M,V,p₀)` only through **`(M⁻¹∇V, M⁻¹p₀)`** (proven, verified 6.2e-16) ⇒ **`M`/`p₀` SELECT, they cannot STORE; the fiber's only storing channel is the local jet of `G = M⁻¹∇V`**; the V↔M degeneracy is an **exact gauge for ANY V** (1e-15 — anharmonicity does not break it; coupling, momentum launch, or the relativistic term do). **Composition: `B_total = K_spatial · B_fiber` (slots × bits, NEVER items × items).** **Parameter ceiling: `B_total ≤ P·b_θ`** — the shipped `PotentialMLP(d=8)` (1377 params) cannot jointly realize the measured spatial capacity and per-well fibers (≥36 864 reals). Fiber bit-counts only with `(σ_rel, N, launches)`; read length is exponential in bits (`relaxation-fiber-capacity`; N76) |
| **honest capacity margin (NEW v2.1, w22)** | The kv=16 MQAR capacity margin over a **matched, rescued, gated GRU** is **2.1× (0.180 vs 0.085)**; 1.7× at kv=8; crossover **kv≈4 gated / kv≈8 linear** (the property pre-exists the gate ⇒ architectural, not a gate artifact). ⛔ **The `primitive-harness §1b` "19×/17×-chance" figure is an un-rescued-GRU artifact — never quote it** (N78). ⚠ Attention wins every cell (0.856±0.170 at kv=16); the edge costs 1.48× GRU wall-clock / 2.16× FLOPs; the SSM comparison carries the Mamba-lite caveat (`gated-write-performance` §3) |
| **mass-timescale exponent (NEW v2.1, w20)** | **`τ ∝ M^0.779 (R²=0.9968)`** on a bit-identical fixed landscape (per-launch `mass_override`; combo arm `M^0.796`). ⛔ **`τ ∝ √M` is RETIRED** — the registered +0.5 exponent failed as registered (wrong damping limit: the probe is heavily damped, where the limit is 1.0) (`mass-visible-objective` recon 2) |

## 2. Cross-short load-bearing claims (approved wording + scope)
| # | claim (approved form) | evidence | scope qualifier (mandatory) | appears in |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| CM-1 | Symplectic volume conservation buys **bounded (BIBO) dynamics, a protected near-flat memory direction, and vacuum robustness under CD training**; the leapfrog structure buys the **latch**; the physics prior **costs raw fit** (unconstrained twin fits ~15× better MSE). Mechanism wording: the measured cost is **contraction-forbidden** (volume), NOT the causal cap (inactive in the arm); reach-pricing is the second, separately-proven restriction (lattice κ-law). **Crossing now MEASURED (w6): the loan is called at ≈700 steps** — the twin leads 1–2 orders to ~500 steps, crosses CLU at ~700, diverges to 196 @5000 vs CLU's bounded 0.20–0.23 plateau. DO claim the crossing + boundedness-by-construction; do NOT claim lowest long-horizon plateau (broken-vol 0.14, LSTM 0.13 < CLU 0.22). This claims **controllability** (which we can evidence) instead of **capability** (which we cannot). ✅ **Also SURVIVING and worth citing prominently: Ramsauer et al. — the modern Hopfield update IS the attention mechanism** ⇒ *"energy-landscape retrieval competes with attention" is a principled claim, not an analogy.* ✅ **Novelty (c) — symplectic structure-preserving test-time retry — is genuinely unoccupied** (no prior art found) *but is "a mechanism in search of a demonstrated benefit."* ⚠ **Narrowed, not retired: "tunable memory permanence"** as a broad claim is taken (**EDEN, NeurIPS 2025** computes escape times analytically with a static/dynamic phase transition, capacity `O(γ^N)`); what survives is **permanence as a per-item design parameter chosen at write time, with three qualitatively distinct modes, one symmetry-protected (`μ²≡0` exactly)** | `scout-dynamical-memory-priorart` §§1–7 (primary sources fetched and quoted where marked) | **✅ UPDATED v2.1 (w21, `scout-benchmark-targeting`): all three retrieval gaps CLOSED.** ~~Ramsauer's constant NOT obtained~~ → **closed** (Lambert-W form via Hu–Wu–Liu Lemma 2.1; primary PDF still unread — cite as *"as restated in"*). ~~UnICORNN unverified~~ → **VERIFIED, and it CUTS AGAINST US**: explicit Hamiltonian, symplectic Euler, time-invertible, **plus a `(NΔt)³` gradient-bound theorem we lack** ⇒ *"symplectic ⇒ long-horizon stability" is OCCUPIED* (see CM-22(b)); surviving differentiators = the relativistic governor · the learned mass spectrum (`τ∝M^0.79`) · energy-based wake–sleep · addressability. ~~SRNN unread~~ → **CLOSED, NOT a preemption** (per-sample L-BFGS-B denoising against a *trajectory-matching* loss, not addressing; consistent with Prop 7 — a good theory paragraph, one-sentence distinction). **NEW must-cite: Titans (NeurIPS 2025)** — test-time GD-with-momentum on an associative-memory loss = discretized damped second-order dynamics, the nearest published neighbour to CLU's write (N77). The binding constraint is the fit→eval generalisation gap across item geometries, not the optimisation budget."* **(c) Launder by omission, on the rival side:** *"Reading a weight-valued memory's byte-matched table through the memory's own projections costs the table 0.26–0.94 against a raw-metric table at the same bytes, because those projections are trained for the recurrence and a single stored value is out of distribution for a head trained on kernel-averaged reads. An audit that runs only that control publishes dividends that do not exist."* **(d) The two-sided ledger asymmetry, as a finding:** *"Every rival's state can be byte-matched to its own table (1.000–1.023); the CLU's provably cannot — its shipped cell sits at 54.56×. A cell whose parameters are its state can be matched on both axes; a cell whose parameters are quadratic in its state cannot, which is why a state-matched GRU would need 79.5 M parameters and a TTT-class cell matches both axes to under 0.1 %."* **(e) The tier-iii first datum, stated in full:** *"The full store runs inside a streaming block on real bytes and trains end-to-end through the trajectory read; the settled-point read's γ- and M-gradients are 0.0 bitwise in-system. At toy scale its stored content changes the model's output by 0.00e+00 bpc, the memory is a net cost against a memory-deleted control (+0.0070 ± 0.0055, 3/3 seeds), and 16× the write budget buys 1.33× well depth and zero acquisition — so the binding constraint is not the write's step budget."* ⚠ **always with: TOY scale, S4 NOT RUN, N94 non-promotable.** **(f) The design identity, from the measured coupling:** *"The one capability a per-slot table cannot imitate — a change of answer when an item the query did not select is deleted — is exponentially suppressed by the very gate that makes the store safe. On our learned store the law holds with R² = 0.995 across a 525× range at a fitted well width of 0.40; at the spacing our shipped configuration achieves (4.3 well widths) the coupling is 1.5e-2, real and the store's, but two orders below the query's own launch noise. The store, the settle and the payload composition work the moment addressing is correct; the blocker is the launch head's assertion, which lands its particle inside the measured capture radius of the right well 94.0 % of the time (0.9402 ± 0.0124, +82 SE) while asserting the right wells at a precision of 0.2356."* ⛔ **DIAGNOSTIC — it hands the read the addressing answer and is NEVER a claim; decisive for attribution only.** **(b) The bound, from two blind spokes on two instruments:** *"The best bijective re-placement of wells onto cue sites — the ceiling on any placement organizer, an exact linear-assignment optimum on the label-free co-occurrence matrix — is 0.3290 ± 0.0074, and an independently computed decodability of the launch points themselves is 0.3449 ± 0.0175: two spokes blind to each other, two instruments, agreeing inside their error bars. Exact-set needs about four of four wells, so even at the unachievable row-max bound of 0.3818 the optimistic exact-set is 0.021, 2.4× below the 0.0504 bar. Both arms are floored by the same cap, so the swap compares nothing — the question is unaskable at this address dimension, and the dial is `d_addr` and the launch head, not the organizer."* ⭐ **The mechanism travels with it: ~2/3 of unseen combinations deflate to a pick-tuple another combination also produces, and it is structural rather than noise (noiseless-launch 0.3424 vs as-launched 0.3372 — σ_q costs 0.005) ⇒ ⛔ a quieter query cannot buy it back.** **(c) The `d_addr` squeeze, and it is stronger than "unaskable at 4":** *"Sweeping the assignment ceiling over address dimensions 4, 6, 8 and 12 squeezes the tier-ii organizer question shut from both ends: below, the ceiling is too low — at dimension 4 the optimistic exact-set is 0.0117, 4.3× under the bar; above, the placement headroom vanishes — the gap between the ceiling and the launch head's own identity assignment falls 0.0851, 0.0375, 0.0109, 0.0000, and at dimension 12 the identity assignment is already the optimal bijection on five of five seeds. The first address dimension whose ceiling clears the bar is also the first at which a placement organizer has nothing left to do."* ⛔ **Never without: the `d = 12` K6 leak is a DECLARED UNKNOWN (measured only at 4, 16 and 32), and the optimistic exact-set is an upper bound on an upper bound.** **(d) The V2a MECHANICS floor — mechanics only, never calibration value:** *"The graded-novelty channel exists where every null channel measurably cannot: AUROC 0.7254 ± 0.0126 against a registered floor of 0.60, with a monotone graded response of −1.740, −1.415 and −1.158 at zero, one and two novel channels, and with the two negatives that can bite — a blank store at 0.5029 and shuffled labels at 0.4972 — beside it. The registered permuted-payload negative cannot bite a depth-keyed channel and measures 0.7903; it is pinned in the test suite as measured rather than quietly dropped."* ⛔ **Never as a calibration win; the swap is NOT-RUN.** **(e) The instrument doctrine the wave earned, and it is standing:** *"A flat anytime curve does not imply an empty store. On the same store, the same physics and the same frozen budget grid, an oracle-addressed read rises 0.0223 → 0.8219 → 0.8711 and then plateaus while the shipped read sits flat at 0.0004 — so flatness licenses only 'the store carries nothing or it cannot be addressed', and the two are separable by exactly this control. All resolvable structure resolves between 50 and 200 steps: beyond that the curve is flat because it is done, not because it is empty."* ⛔ **The read-compute ratio (≈2 160× the physics arm, 585× vs the static null at matched bytes and atoms) is labelled and never primary.** **(f) The family-construction law, which outlives the wave:** *"A compositional family whose payload targets sit outside the basins that must capture them is unreadable by construction, for reasons that have nothing to do with organization: `‖v_j‖ < capture_radius ≲ min-well-spacing`. The read was launching with its payload block pinned to zero while every well's target sat at norm 1.0 against a measured capture radius of 0.8535 — a launch-to-target-over-capture ratio of 1.172, uncovered on every query, channel and seed. Repairing it against a ratio target registered before the sweep moves the settle's occupancy dividend from −0.1567 ± 0.0052 to −0.0015 ± 0.0026, a tie — so four waves of 'the settle destroys launch information' was a reach artifact."* ⛔ **Three qualifiers travel: information-NEUTRAL not positive · an UNORGANIZED store · a CANDIDATE mechanism for §A31.2, NOT a discharge.** **(g) The inversion at a second level of the stack:** *"Label information entering the organizer is harmful to address geometry, and more sharply than at the encoder: a weakly-supervised organizer scores 0.1199 settle-occupancy precision against 0.2125 for the label-free arm, about twelve standard errors, where the encoder-level inversion was 0.1406 ± 0.0508. The label-free ruling is vindicated by its own registered ablation — and the uncomfortable half is that every organizer loses to no organizer at all (0.2329 with none), so the shipped organizer term is net-harmful at this cell and does not carry forward unredesigned."* ⛔ **MECHANICS statistics, never a swap number.** **(h) The gate doctrine, fourth instance in four waves and this one self-reported:** *"A gate leg must be scored on an arm capable of producing both outcomes. Ours was circular — the launder leg was defined on the arm including its training signal, the organizer belonged to the spoke that was gated on the leg — so the leg was scored on a store that had never been organized, at the floor, and a vacuous test was made a blocking condition. The repair was registered mechanically before the re-run, with a numeric abstention threshold of 0.01, and generalized rather than special-cased — which immediately caught two further legs abstaining that a targeted patch would have missed."* | `c2w11-physics-organizer` (M3/M6/M7/M8, the oracle-addressed legs, K0/K1); `c2w11-organizer-swap-nulls` §3/§3.1/§6/§7/§8 (the null table, `null*`, the ceiling + collision control, F5, the V3 curve); `c2w11-substrate-and-kills`; `.claude/outputs/c2w11/DADDR-CEILING-PROBE.json`; charter **§A42.1–§A42.12 · §A43.1 · §A38 · Add.16 §A44–§A45**; the `2026-08-12` `[C2W11]` CAMPAIGN-CLOSE §10 entry; **N302–N312 (✰)** | ⛔ **(a)** is DIAGNOSTIC and never a claim · ⛔ **(b)** never as *"the organizer swap failed"* — the swap is a DECLARED NOT-RUN · ⛔ **(c)** never without the `d = 12` unknown and the out-of-class bound · ⛔ **(d)** mechanics only, never calibration value · ⛔ **(e)/(h)** are methodology and license no performance claim · ⛔ **(f)** carries its three qualifiers · ⛔ **(g)** never as a V1 swap number · ⚠ **Nothing here moves the score sentence: external benchmarks won = ZERO, and the tier-ii VALUE verdict is UNREACHED for a fifth consecutive wave — attributably, with a bound and a named dial, and NEVER refuted.** ⛔ **All of it is TOY and the substrate is formally demoted (Add.16 §A44.1).** | the tier-ii / memory-architecture write-up; the instrument-methodology appendix; **the C3 charter (Add.16 §A45 carries the exoneration + the launch-head bound + the design-rule corpus)**; any launch-head / read-protocol section |

## 3.1 Editorial & revision conventions (standing) — **NEW at v2.10, 2026-08-01 (`doc-curator-c2w4-fold`)**

*Why this section exists: the program had no home for editorial conventions, so a Head ruling about file naming had nowhere to live except the charter and one §10 entry. Conventions that bind a paper-writer belong beside the claims that bind it.*

- ⭐⭐ **THE r-CONVENTION (Head ruling **A18.5**, 2026-08-01 — BINDING for B′).** B′'s revisions are **`draft-r2.md`, `draft-r3.md`, …** — ⛔ **never `draft-v2.md`.** **Rationale, recorded because it is the whole reason:** *"v2" collides with the **V2 short*** (the memory-as-broken-symmetry paper), and a filename that reads as another paper's name is a citation hazard in a program with five shorts. **Applied:** `draft-v2.md` → **`draft-r2.md`** renamed by the C2W5 Hub, 2026-08-01, **no content change**, recorded in `.claude/papers/bprime/CHANGELOG.md`; the post-referee revision is **`draft-r3.md`**. ✅ **Head nod given** (`[C2W5]` decision queue 3).
- ⚠ **Precedence, stated so it is not re-litigated:** A18.5 (**Head**) **governs over** `[C2W4-CLOSE]` ruling 5 (**Hub**, `draft-vN` as the general convention) — *a Head ruling supersedes a Hub ruling*. ⛔ **Scope of the supersession is B′ only:** the `draft-vN` form is **retained for non-B′ multi-revision papers** unless the Head says otherwise. **`draft-v1.md` is not renamed** — it is the historical artefact of the number-freeze pass and is cited as such.
- ⚠ **The "CLU" name-debut question is NOT settled here** — it is absorbed into the **post-C2W5 shorts re-pass (A18.7)**, venue/timing the Head's exclusively. ⛔ **Until then "CLU-former" remains a PLACEHOLDER** (§0.7, §0.8, CM-26(xx), N190) — the naming convention above governs *revision suffixes*, not names.
- ✅ **Corrections to published spoke reports are dated erratum BANNERS, never body edits (C-3, ratified 2026-08-01); task files are historical work orders and stay unedited (C-4).** ⭐ **And the reversal-sweep rule from §3: when the Head reverses a ruling, the sweep covers the charter, the handover, THIS FILE and every agent brief — with a named owner at the review that makes the reversal** (N210).

- ⭐⭐ **THE SUBSTRATE-SCOPE SENTENCE — a new item on the A18.7 shorts re-pass checklist (charter §A20.5, added 2026-08-01, `doc-curator-c2w5-fold`).** ⛔ **Every short states its substrate scope, in its own voice, once:** *"these laws govern the store; end-to-end performance additionally depends on the encoder, measured separately, φ-bytes ledgered."* **Why it is a convention and not a preference:** ⭐ **the program's laws are store-level, and every end-to-end null it has measured has had an encoder in the path** — the read-in `φ` (§11.11), the read-out `ψ` (⛔ **which C2W5 measured compressing the payload 7–25×**, N222), or both. **A short that omits the clause invites a reviewer to read a store-level law as an end-to-end claim, and a store-level null as an end-to-end refutation.** ⚠ **The re-pass itself is sequenced after C2W5 and its venue/timing are the Head's exclusively**; the checklist lives in `future_work.md`'s A18.7 entry, and this is its editorial-rule half.
- ⛔ **THE §A20.2 RE-LABELLING IS AN EDITORIAL RULE AS WELL AS A NEVER-QUOTE.** Every draft, figure caption, appendix and docstring says **"the `P`-particle occupancy read protocol is refuted at `P = 4`"** — ⛔ **never "the compositional family is refuted"**, and never a tier-ii physics verdict (the organizer swap was not run). ⚠ **The sweep for this reversal follows N210's sharpened rule — documents, filesystem AND tracked code** (one live site was found in `chlu/experiments/exp_null_arms.py`'s module docstring and is flagged to the Hub).

**~~The original Hub-recorded owed block follows, struck as superseded:~~** Evidence: `.claude/outputs/{clu-retrieval-demo,clu-memory-architecture,clu-latent-io-audit,scout-dynamical-memory-priorart}.md`; handover §10 entry 2026-07-21.

1. **NEW CM candidate — the memory loop works under design.** Hand-built write→address→retrieve: payload retrieved to **9.98e-4**, linear read **1.000** @2 items / **0.992** @8, **blank-landscape control 0.469 ≈ chance**; durability **flat to 1200 steps**; three write modes co-realized with **zero cross-corruption (4.17e-7)** and half-life **66.87 vs 2ln2/γ = 69.31**. ⚠ **Everything is DESIGNED, nothing emergent** (N46 precedent — do not let this be read as emergence). ⚠ **A full-state read scores 1.000 on a BLANK landscape** — it reads the address back; **any retrieval claim reading the address plane is decorative.**
2. **NEW CM candidate — hard read isolation (Prop 2, proven+verified).** Heavy particles are **provably confined to their home basin** (`M* = p₀²/2h`, verified within one grid cell). **Softmax mixes every key; a sub-barrier particle CANNOT read a foreign item.** This is a genuine structural advantage over attention and is currently the program's strongest defensible differentiator.
3. **CM-5/N7 REQUIRE AMENDMENT — mechanism wrong, conclusion right.** `log_mass` is **not** frozen (moves +3.56; gradient **8,200× larger per-parameter** than `V_θ`) ⇒ **N7's "masses stay at init" is WRONG on the eval/CAFE path and D1's premise is INVERTED there.** The spectrum is nonetheless inert **by common-mode runaway** (`M_max/M_min` 1.153 < init 1.265). Amend wording wherever CM-5/N7 assert frozen masses or a null-direction mechanism.
4. **D1 partition constant is K-dependent — correct every downstream use.** `η_m/(η_m + K·η_k)`, not `1/11` (K=2 → 1/21, matched to 13 digits). Anyone reusing `anti-collapse-characterization` §5.1 in a multi-item setting must use the K-form.
5. **CM-16 / SDR sharpening — SDR is a PREREQUISITE, with a hard limit.** Confirmed: regularizer/design supplies *existence/non-degeneracy*; the loss supplies *assignment* (T2/T3). **But mode-(a) permanent latches cannot come from ANY finite-λ soft regularizer** (R-4 ⇒ μ²(λ)>0 ⇒ finite half-life; designed-vs-emergent flatness gap is 12 orders). **Permanent memory must be designed-in structural blocks.**
6. **Capacity claim must be stated as THREE REGIMES, never one number.** Barrier-protected (100% selectivity) · **washboard death zone (K≈16, selectivity 0.49) — where v5-gate's emergent 1–1.6-bit register lives, i.e. an EMERGENT CHLU is stuck in regime 2** · continuum register (K≥24, written angle retained **exactly** (drift 0.000), selectivity 0.96, tens of bits). **Any "transformer competitor" claim is CONDITIONAL ON DESIGNED-IN STRUCTURE.** ⚠ **Do NOT quote the 8-item ceiling as CLU's capacity** — 2-D-ring artifact; packing bound is `(1+2R/w)^d`, exponential in `d`.
7. **POSITIONING CLAIMS TO RETIRE (scout-verified).** (a) *"retrieval is a rollout, not a weighted sum"* as novelty — **published: Kong, Brewer & Lai, Nat. Comms. 2024**, must be cited. (b) *"a retention guarantee attention has no analogue for"* — **HiPPO-LegS has one**, with `O(tL/√N)` error and **gradient `Θ(1/t)`**. (c) *"continuity is our NTM/DNC escape"* — **false, both were already soft.** (d) **"competitor to the transformer" is currently indefensible**; use **physics-structured associative memory with designed retention**. ⚠ **Open unanswered challenge: HiPPO-LegS Prop 3 is exactly timescale-equivariant and has NO `dt`** — a reviewer will ask why a *learned* mass spectrum beats provable invariance.
8. **CM-3 discipline, new instance.** The `dt` units bug means every prior CLU-on-CAFE number was produced with **`H`≈`K` (corr 0.999996)** and a **98.3% ballistic** rollout — i.e. the potential barely participated. **All FD00x numbers are provisional pending the `dt` fix and a re-run.**
